Network state, 30 Sep 2026

Long-term changes are part of the model, and the September timetables already run them. Short works, and works that start after the vehicle day, are left out.

modelledNa Karolině street roundabouts: lines 21, 25, 81, 92

From 25 May 2026 until further notice. Lines 21 and 81 are diverted via Škrobálkova, Vratimovská, Lihovarská and Šenovská streets, and lines 25 and 92 via Frýdecká street. Důl Alexander and Hradní náměstí are closed. At Hranečník, lines 21 and 81 leave from stand 6. The September timetables run the diversion. Since 2 Feb 2026 the Na Karolině bridge over the Ostravice is being demolished and rebuilt: lines 21 and 81 are diverted, and line 25 was added. Both are until further notice (checked 20 Oct 2026).

modelledNáměstí Republiky rebuild: lines 50, 73, 99 and the interchange

From 2 Feb 2026 until further notice, 28. října street is closed towards the centre. Line 50 runs via Nádraží střed towards Porážková, 73 via Náměstí Republiky stand C, and 99 via Českobratrská and Konzervatoř, skipping Náměstí Republiky, Karolina, Elektra and Stodolní towards Hladnov. From 17 Sep 2026, buses 25, 37, 71 and 73 board at the provisional stop Náměstí Republiky C on Českobratrská.

modelledLine 67, Vrablovec bridge in Ludgeřovice

25 May – 20 Nov 2026: diverted both ways via Hlučínská, Lipová and Vrablovecká streets between Petřkovice, náměstí and Ludgeřovice, Vrablovec, střed. Longer than the modelled period, so it is modelled as it runs. Checked 20 Oct 2026: it still runs until 20 Nov. When it ends, line 67 goes back to its regular route.

modelledKlimkovice, centrum stops moved: lines 59, 64, 76

20 Feb – 12 Dec 2026, during the closure of the southern part of Požárnická street. Klimkovice, centrum is served at Klimkovice, výkupní závod on Lidická street (before the roundabout towards Svinov and Vítkovice, past it towards Hýlov), and the routes change accordingly. The CIS JŘ timetable still lists the regular stops (centrum, Požárnická), so in the game route the three lines via Lidická street. Termini and minutes are unchanged, so the Simutrans schedules are as shown.

not modelledLine 22, Dědičná street repair

5–16 Oct 2026, with trolleybus 106 and night line 113: one direction was diverted. It was short-term and has ended (checked 20 Oct 2026), so line 22 keeps its regular route.

not modelledLine 45, Most Mládeže stop moved

From 1 Oct 2026 until further notice, during the Rudná × Plzeňská bridge works: the stop towards Městský stadion is 160 m further on, at a provisional stop on Rudná street. It is a stop move only, with no change of route or times, so it is not modelled.

Each vehicle shows its number and depot, full type and length, and pak128 core name. Green border: seen on this line in the live feed at the peak. Blue border: seen on this line in the evening before, not yet confirmed at the peak. Dashed border: designed, not recorded, with the reason. Thick orange border: the owner's choice of type, in place of a bus seen there. Dimmed, dotted: not needed, a bus from the line's history that was free that day, shown but not counted.

Approximate schedules to check (5)

These rows have an irregular real timetable, so they get an even approximate pattern with the same rate at both ends of the line. Each one has more slots in 15:30–17:30 than real departures, so the extra slots would run empty or spread the buses thinner. Where a slower pattern exists, it is shown with its own worst deviation. If you pick it, switch both ends of that Simutrans line together. The rows are also marked on their line cards.

Circuit legs off the timetable (11)

Circuits across branches (or across two paths) run on the line's fitted slots. These legs have slots more than 3 min from any real departure in the line's window, because the real timetable is irregular there. The alternative is the separate branch lines, at the bus count shown.

Stop coverage

568 stops are served by the sheet's lines between 13:00 and 19:00. 566 of them are on a modelled Simutrans line, including every path of the lines that alternate between two (28, 32, 49 and ODIS 531) and the Havířov Město loop of ODIS 441. The 2 below are only on single trips.

StopLineTrips 13:00–19:00Why not modelled
Heřmanice, Dolina2218:30 Hranečník → Heřmanice, Důl Heřmanice; 18:55 Heřmanice, Důl Heřmanice → Hranečník2 trips in 13:00–19:00, outside the line's afternoon peak pattern; local lines are modelled at their afternoon peak
Heřmanice, Parcelní2218:30 Hranečník → Heřmanice, Důl Heřmanice; 18:55 Heřmanice, Důl Heřmanice → Hranečník2 trips in 13:00–19:00, outside the line's afternoon peak pattern; local lines are modelled at their afternoon peak

Lines

Branches. With 47 lines, branches are found by rule instead of by hand. Within 15:00–18:00, each pair of termini a line serves is its own Simutrans line when it has at least 6 trips (about 3 each way), a departure in the 16 o'clock hour, and no garage at either end. The busiest pair always counts. Smaller pairs (extensions, short workings, garage runs) are not separate lines, because “Also at” cannot change the route; their minutes are folded into the main row from the same terminus. Lines 49 and 54 end at Přívoz, sad Boženy Němcové and start back from Moravská Ostrava, Vozovna trolejbusů, the next stop, so those two count as one terminus. Where a line has two or more Simutrans lines, they are lettered A, B, C by trips.

Buses needed. “Needed” follows one bus round the branch at 16:00, with 3 min layovers or the terminus's own shorter timetabled turnaround. A round trip that would wait longer than 30 min, or 1.5 headways on a sparse line, for a return trip is skipped, because by then the buses leave as another line. Where a line has extensions or short workings that are not separate Simutrans lines, its main branch is topped up to the number of buses the timetable chains together at 16:00, so their buses are not lost. Lines with no complete round trip around 16:00 use the chained count, and at least one bus.

Headway and runtime come from the timetable for 15:00–17:00. “Leaves 16:xx” lists that terminus's departure minutes in the 16 o'clock hour.

The Simutrans schedule holds the values to type into the Scheduling tab of each terminus entry, with the calendar switched on. “Departure every 20, Offset 3, Also at 13” gives :03 :13 :23 :33 :43 :53. Each row is the longest stretch of the afternoon around the peak that one pattern reproduces exactly (shown as “exact from–to”). Bus timetables are often irregular, so many rows use a 60-minute cycle with several “Also at” values, and some are exact for only an hour. Rows marked “not scheduled” are variants with too few trips, or depot runs; their minutes are folded into the main row from the same terminus.

Departures one minute early. Every terminus is set as a loop, including block turns and turning points, as agreed for the tram, trolleybus and bus sheets. Each Offset and “Also at” value is 1 min before the timetable departure, so the bus clears the terminus on time. The real minutes are listed under each entry, and the “Leaves 16:xx” column shows the real timetable.

Exception approved by the owner. An exception the owner approved for this case (20 Oct 2026): local lines are otherwise modelled only at their afternoon peak. Three runs a day go from Kunčice, Nová huť garáže (a public stop, not a DPO depot) to Hulváky via Vítkovice, U chladičů: 6:08, 14:08, 22:13. They are the stop's only service and repeat every 480, a shift pattern. So they are their own Simutrans line every 480 from Nová huť at 14:08 (22:13 is 5 min after its 22:08 slot), and the bus returns empty, untimed; no trip runs back that way. Core points: Kunčice, Nová huť garáže, Vítkovice, U chladičů, Hulváky. The timetables sit on the terminals only (bus schedule method, 20 Oct 2026).

How this was built

The roster comes from the live feed. DPO's live feed (dashboard.dpo.cz, vehicleType “bus”) is polled every minute over the afternoon of Wednesday 30 Sep 2026. Each bus's line and course come from its first sighting between 15:50 and 16:15. The feed keeps no history, and seznam-autobusu.cz has almost no DPO bus records (3 on Fri 25 Sep), so the observation is the only per-vehicle source. That is 167 buses, against a timetable need of 182. 26 buses are designed where a Simutrans line had fewer buses than it needs, each with the reason on its row.

Timetable. CIS JŘ bus export (CIS JŘ bus JDF export of 28 Sep 2026): every line numbered 915xxx whose operator is DPO, found by scanning the export, 57 lines in all. Evaluated for Wednesday 30 Sep 2026 with the shared version rule. 47 lines run in the afternoon and are on the sheet. The others are listed under “Not in the afternoon peak”.

Machinery. The same code as the Ostrava tram and trolleybus sheets: the JDF reader, round-trip sizing (cycle_need with the 30-minute guard from the trolleybus sheet) and the schedule fit (runfit).

pak128 objects. Each bus is matched to its object in VZ-DPOstrava-bus. The Urbino objects come in generations: s3 is the third generation, in blue-yellow (dpomodrozluta), and s4 the fourth, in turquoise (dpotyrkysova). Urbino 12 III CNG and Urbino 18 III CNG have their own CNG objects, and Urbino 12 III CNG and Urbino 18 III also come in turquoise for the cars repainted in DPO's blue with white stripe. The Urbino 10 is blue-yellow only. The 24 electric Urbino 12 IV (5008–5031) use their own battery object, SolarisUrbino12_s4_electric, in turquoise. It has been installed since 10 Oct 2026. Real paints the object does not have, and advertising wraps, are listed as nearest in the Pak128 objects table.

Sources: CIS JŘ bus JDF, DPO live dashboard, DPO bus register, DPO electric bus register, DPO výluky.

All lines at Hranečník (18)

Every bus line with a stop at Hranečník on Wednesday 30 Sep 2026: the 10 DPO lines (their cards are in Lines above) and 8 ODIS lines of other operators, whose cards follow in the same style. ODIS lines: CIS JŘ national JDF (the same export as the DPO lines), versions valid on the day; schedule, branches and needs by the same rules as the DPO lines. They are not in DPO's live feed. Their buses are the ones seen on the ODIS live map (mpvnet.cz) on Tue 20 Oct 2026, a different day from the DPO cards: ODIS live map (mpvnet.cz), polled by the Havířov/Karviná/Orlová session, one snapshot a minute 13:05–19:30. Each bus at the line's peak gives its exact trip (the map's trip number is the timetable's), and it goes to the Simutrans line whose slot that trip runs, so the 442/441 circuit holds buses seen on 441. 25 buses are seen; 2 more are designed where none was seen, a real bus from the line's own records, not seen on the map that afternoon. Their pak objects are in VZ-ODIS-bus (installed 20 Oct 2026): each bus has its own type in its real livery, 3ČSAD white-green or Havířovsko white. Trams 4, 10, 12, 13 and trolleybuses 101, 107 also serve Hranečník; they are on the tram and trolleybus sheets.

So :05 → :32 and :40 → :17 are one circuit, Simutrans line 442/441; the rest of 442 (:10 :20 :50 out) is an exact hourly line with three slots. 10 buses for 441 + 442 against 11 with :05/:10 → :32/:47.
